## 二、测试框架代码

```c
/*============================================================================
 * test_framework.h - 测试框架
 *============================================================================*/
#ifndef __TEST_FRAMEWORK_H
#define __TEST_FRAMEWORK_H

#include <stdint.h>
#include <stdbool.h>
#include <stdio.h>
#include <math.h>

/*============================================================================
 * 测试结果定义
 *============================================================================*/
typedef enum {
    TEST_PASS = 0,
    TEST_FAIL,
    TEST_SKIP,
    TEST_ERROR
} TestResult_t;

typedef struct {
    const char*     name;           // 测试名称
    const char*     description;    // 测试描述
    TestResult_t    result;         // 测试结果
    float           expected;       // 期望值
    float           actual;         // 实际值
    float           tolerance;      // 允许误差
    float           error_percent;  // 误差百分比
    uint32_t        duration_ms;    // 测试耗时
    char            message[128];   // 结果消息
} TestCase_t;

typedef struct {
    const char*     suite_name;     // 测试套件名称
    uint32_t        total;          // 总测试数
    uint32_t        passed;         // 通过数
    uint32_t        failed;         // 失败数
    uint32_t        skipped;        // 跳过数
    uint32_t        errors;         // 错误数
    uint32_t        start_time;     // 开始时间
    uint32_t        end_time;       // 结束时间
} TestSuite_t;

/*============================================================================
 * 测试宏定义
 *============================================================================*/
#define TEST_ASSERT(condition, msg) \
    do { \
        if (!(condition)) { \
            snprintf(g_current_test->message, sizeof(g_current_test->message), \
                     "ASSERT FAILED: %s (line %d)", msg, __LINE__); \
            g_current_test->result = TEST_FAIL; \
            return; \
        } \
    } while(0)

#define TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL(expected, actual, tolerance) \
    do { \
        float _exp = (expected); \
        float _act = (actual); \
        float _tol = (tolerance); \
        float _err = fabsf(_act - _exp); \
        g_current_test->expected = _exp; \
        g_current_test->actual = _act; \
        g_current_test->tolerance = _tol; \
        if (_exp != 0) { \
            g_current_test->error_percent = (_err / fabsf(_exp)) * 100.0f; \
        } \
        if (_err > _tol) { \
            snprintf(g_current_test->message, sizeof(g_current_test->message), \
                     "Expected: %.6e, Actual: %.6e, Error: %.2f%%", \
                     _exp, _act, g_current_test->error_percent); \
            g_current_test->result = TEST_FAIL; \
            return; \
        } \
    } while(0)

#define TEST_ASSERT_RANGE(value, min, max) \
    do { \
        float _val = (value); \
        float _min = (min); \
        float _max = (max); \
        if (_val < _min || _val > _max) { \
            snprintf(g_current_test->message, sizeof(g_current_test->message), \
                     "Value %.6e out of range [%.6e, %.6e]", _val, _min, _max); \
            g_current_test->result = TEST_FAIL; \
            return; \
        } \
    } while(0)

/*============================================================================
 * 测试函数类型
 *============================================================================*/
typedef void (*TestFunc_t)(void);

/*============================================================================
 * 全局变量
 *============================================================================*/
extern TestCase_t* g_current_test;
extern TestSuite_t g_test_suite;

/*============================================================================
 * API 函数
 *============================================================================*/
void Test_Init(const char* suite_name);
void Test_Run(const char* name, const char* desc, TestFunc_t func);
void Test_PrintReport(void);
void Test_Delay(uint32_t ms);
float Test_GetStableCurrent(uint32_t samples, uint32_t interval_ms);

#endif /* __TEST_FRAMEWORK_H */
/*============================================================================
 * test_framework.c - 测试框架实现
 *============================================================================*/
#include "test_framework.h"
#include "current_meter.h"

TestCase_t* g_current_test = NULL;
TestSuite_t g_test_suite;
static TestCase_t g_test_case;

void Test_Init(const char* suite_name)
{
    memset(&g_test_suite, 0, sizeof(g_test_suite));
    g_test_suite.suite_name = suite_name;
    g_test_suite.start_time = HAL_GetTick();
    
    printf("\n");
    printf("╔══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╗\n");
    printf("║                    测试套件: %-30s║\n", suite_name);
    printf("╠══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╣\n");
    printf("║  开始时间: %lu ms                                            ║\n", g_test_suite.start_time);
    printf("╚══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╝\n");
    printf("\n");
}

void Test_Run(const char* name, const char* desc, TestFunc_t func)
{
    // 初始化测试用例
    memset(&g_test_case, 0, sizeof(g_test_case));
    g_test_case.name = name;
    g_test_case.description = desc;
    g_test_case.result = TEST_PASS;
    g_current_test = &g_test_case;
    
    uint32_t start = HAL_GetTick();
    
    printf("┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐\n");
    printf("│ 测试: %-55s│\n", name);
    printf("│ 描述: %-55s│\n", desc);
    printf("├──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤\n");
    
    // 执行测试
    func();
    
    g_test_case.duration_ms = HAL_GetTick() - start;
    g_test_suite.total++;
    
    // 统计结果
    switch (g_test_case.result) {
        case TEST_PASS:
            g_test_suite.passed++;
            printf("│ 结果: ✅ PASS                                                │\n");
            break;
        case TEST_FAIL:
            g_test_suite.failed++;
            printf("│ 结果: ❌ FAIL                                                │\n");
            printf("│ 原因: %-55s│\n", g_test_case.message);
            break;
        case TEST_SKIP:
            g_test_suite.skipped++;
            printf("│ 结果: ⏭️ SKIP                                                │\n");
            break;
        case TEST_ERROR:
            g_test_suite.errors++;
            printf("│ 结果: ⚠️ ERROR                                               │\n");
            break;
    }
    
    printf("│ 耗时: %lu ms                                                  │\n", g_test_case.duration_ms);
    printf("└──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘\n\n");
}

void Test_PrintReport(void)
{
    g_test_suite.end_time = HAL_GetTick();
    uint32_t total_time = g_test_suite.end_time - g_test_suite.start_time;
    float pass_rate = (g_test_suite.total > 0) ? 
                      (float)g_test_suite.passed / g_test_suite.total * 100.0f : 0;
    
    printf("\n");
    printf("╔══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╗\n");
    printf("║                        测试报告                              ║\n");
    printf("╠══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╣\n");
    printf("║  测试套件: %-50s║\n", g_test_suite.suite_name);
    printf("╠══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╣\n");
    printf("║  总测试数: %-5lu    通过: %-5lu    失败: %-5lu              ║\n",
           g_test_suite.total, g_test_suite.passed, g_test_suite.failed);
    printf("║  跳过: %-5lu        错误: %-5lu                              ║\n",
           g_test_suite.skipped, g_test_suite.errors);
    printf("║  通过率: %.1f%%                                               ║\n", pass_rate);
    printf("║  总耗时: %lu ms                                              ║\n", total_time);
    printf("╠══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╣\n");
    
    if (g_test_suite.failed == 0 && g_test_suite.errors == 0) {
        printf("║                    ✅ 所有测试通过！                         ║\n");
    } else {
        printf("║                    ❌ 存在失败的测试                         ║\n");
    }
    
    printf("╚══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╝\n");
}

float Test_GetStableCurrent(uint32_t samples, uint32_t interval_ms)
{
    float sum = 0;
    for (uint32_t i = 0; i < samples; i++) {
        CM_Process();
        sum += CM_GetCurrent_A();
        HAL_Delay(interval_ms);
    }
    return sum / samples;
}

void Test_Delay(uint32_t ms)
{
    uint32_t start = HAL_GetTick();
    while ((HAL_GetTick() - start) < ms) {
        CM_Process();  // 保持电流计运行
    }
}
```

## 三、单元测试

### 3.1 INA229 驱动测试

```c
/*============================================================================
 * test_ina229_driver.c - INA229 驱动单元测试
 *============================================================================*/
#include "test_framework.h"
#include "ina229.h"

/*============================================================================
 * TC-DRV-001: 设备ID读取测试
 *============================================================================*/
void Test_INA229_DeviceID(void)
{
    uint16_t mfg_id = INA229_ReadReg16(INA229_REG_MFG_ID);
    uint16_t dev_id = INA229_ReadReg16(INA229_REG_DEV_ID);
    
    // 制造商ID应为 0x5449 ("TI")
    TEST_ASSERT(mfg_id == 0x5449, "Manufacturer ID mismatch");
    
    // 设备ID高12位应为 0x229
    TEST_ASSERT((dev_id >> 4) == 0x229, "Device ID mismatch");
    
    snprintf(g_current_test->message, sizeof(g_current_test->message),
             "MFG_ID=0x%04X, DEV_ID=0x%04X", mfg_id, dev_id);
}

/*============================================================================
 * TC-DRV-002: 寄存器读写测试
 *============================================================================*/
void Test_INA229_RegisterRW(void)
{
    // 测试 CONFIG 寄存器读写
    uint16_t test_value = 0x1234;
    uint16_t original = INA229_ReadReg16(INA229_REG_CONFIG);
    
    INA229_WriteReg16(INA229_REG_CONFIG, test_value);
    uint16_t readback = INA229_ReadReg16(INA229_REG_CONFIG);
    
    // 恢复原值
    INA229_WriteReg16(INA229_REG_CONFIG, original);
    
    // 注意: CONFIG 寄存器某些位是只读的，只比较可写位
    uint16_t writable_mask = 0x0010;  // ADCRANGE 位
    TEST_ASSERT((readback & writable_mask) == (test_value & writable_mask),
                "Register read/write mismatch");
}

/*============================================================================
 * TC-DRV-003: ADC配置测试
 *============================================================================*/
void Test_INA229_ADCConfig(void)
{
    // 配置 ADC: 连续模式，540μs转换时间，16次平均
    uint16_t adc_config = INA229_MODE_CONT_ALL |
                          INA229_VBUSCT_540US |
                          INA229_VSHCT_540US |
                          INA229_VTCT_540US |
                          INA229_AVG_16;
    
    INA229_WriteReg16(INA229_REG_ADC_CONFIG, adc_config);
    uint16_t readback = INA229_ReadReg16(INA229_REG_ADC_CONFIG);
    
    TEST_ASSERT(readback == adc_config, "ADC config mismatch");
}

/*============================================================================
 * TC-DRV-004: 分流电压读取测试 (零输入)
 *============================================================================*/
void Test_INA229_ShuntVoltageZero(void)
{
    // 前提: 无电流输入
    // 期望: 分流电压接近0
    
    HAL_Delay(100);  // 等待ADC稳定
    
    int32_t raw = INA229_ReadReg24(INA229_REG_VSHUNT);
    float vshunt_uv = raw * 0.3125f;  // LSB = 312.5nV
    
    // 允许 ±50μV 偏移
    TEST_ASSERT_RANGE(vshunt_uv, -50.0f, 50.0f);
    
    snprintf(g_current_test->message, sizeof(g_current_test->message),
             "Vshunt = %.2f μV (zero input)", vshunt_uv);
}

/*============================================================================
 * TC-DRV-005: 总线电压读取测试
 *============================================================================*/
void Test_INA229_BusVoltage(void)
{
    // 读取总线电压
    int32_t raw = INA229_ReadReg24(INA229_REG_VBUS);
    float vbus = (raw >> 4) * 0.0001953125f;  // LSB = 195.3125μV
    
    // 假设被测电路电压在 3~24V 范围内
    TEST_ASSERT_RANGE(vbus, 2.5f, 30.0f);
    
    snprintf(g_current_test->message, sizeof(g_current_test->message),
             "Vbus = %.3f V", vbus);
}

/*============================================================================
 * TC-DRV-006: 温度读取测试
 *============================================================================*/
void Test_INA229_Temperature(void)
{
    int32_t raw = INA229_ReadReg16(INA229_REG_DIETEMP);
    float temp = raw * 0.0078125f;  // LSB = 7.8125m°C
    
    // 温度应在合理范围内 (0~60°C)
    TEST_ASSERT_RANGE(temp, 0.0f, 60.0f);
    
    snprintf(g_current_test->message, sizeof(g_current_test->message),
             "Temperature = %.2f °C", temp);
}

/*============================================================================
 * TC-DRV-007: ALERT 功能测试
 *============================================================================*/
void Test_INA229_Alert(void)
{
    // 配置过压警报阈值 (设置一个很低的值以触发警报)
    INA229_WriteReg16(INA229_REG_SOVL, 100);  // 很低的阈值
    
    // 使能警报
    uint16_t diag = INA229_DIAG_ALATCH | INA229_DIAG_SHNTOL;
    INA229_WriteReg16(INA229_REG_DIAG_ALRT, diag);
    
    HAL_Delay(10);
    
    // 读取警报状态
    uint16_t status = INA229_ReadReg16(INA229_REG_DIAG_ALRT);
    
    // 恢复正常阈值
    INA229_WriteReg16(INA229_REG_SOVL, 0x7FFF);
    
    // 检查警报是否触发 (如果有电流的话)
    // 这里只验证寄存器可读写
    TEST_ASSERT((status & INA229_DIAG_ALATCH) != 0, "Alert latch not set");
}

/*============================================================================
 * 运行所有 INA229 驱动测试
 *============================================================================*/
void Test_INA229_Driver_Suite(void)
{
    Test_Init("INA229 驱动测试");
    
    Test_Run("TC-DRV-001", "设备ID读取", Test_INA229_DeviceID);
    Test_Run("TC-DRV-002", "寄存器读写", Test_INA229_RegisterRW);
    Test_Run("TC-DRV-003", "ADC配置", Test_INA229_ADCConfig);
    Test_Run("TC-DRV-004", "分流电压零点", Test_INA229_ShuntVoltageZero);
    Test_Run("TC-DRV-005", "总线电压读取", Test_INA229_BusVoltage);
    Test_Run("TC-DRV-006", "温度读取", Test_INA229_Temperature);
    Test_Run("TC-DRV-007", "ALERT功能", Test_INA229_Alert);
    
    Test_PrintReport();
}
```

### 3.2 校准算法测试

```c
/*============================================================================
 * test_calibration.c - 校准算法单元测试
 *============================================================================*/
#include "test_framework.h"
#include "calibration.h"

/*============================================================================
 * TC-CAL-001: 校准数据初始化测试
 *============================================================================*/
void Test_CAL_Init(void)
{
    CAL_Init();
    
    const CalibrationData_t* cal = CAL_GetData();
    
    // 检查默认值
    TEST_ASSERT(cal->magic == CAL_MAGIC_NUMBER, "Magic number mismatch");
    TEST_ASSERT(cal->version == CAL_VERSION, "Version mismatch");
    
    // 检查默认增益为1.0
    for (int i = 0; i < CAL_NUM_RANGES; i++) {
        TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL(1.0f, cal->ranges[i].gain, 0.001f);
    }
}

/*============================================================================
 * TC-CAL-002: 偏移校准测试
 *============================================================================*/
void Test_CAL_OffsetCorrection(void)
{
    // 模拟数据: 原始值有偏移
    float raw_current = 0.000005f;  // 5μA 偏移
    float offset = 0.000005f;
    float gain = 1.0f;
    
    // 应用校准
    float corrected = (raw_current - offset) * gain;
    
    // 期望结果接近0
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL(0.0f, corrected, 0.0000001f);
}

/*============================================================================
 * TC-CAL-003: 增益校准测试
 *============================================================================*/
void Test_CAL_GainCorrection(void)
{
    // 模拟数据: 原始值有增益误差
    float raw_current = 0.00095f;   // 测量值 950μA
    float reference = 0.001f;       // 实际值 1000μA
    float offset = 0.0f;
    
    // 计算增益
    float gain = reference / (raw_current - offset);
    
    // 应用校准
    float corrected = (raw_current - offset) * gain;
    
    // 期望结果等于参考值
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL(reference, corrected, reference * 0.001f);
}

/*============================================================================
 * TC-CAL-004: 线性度校准测试
 *============================================================================*/
void Test_CAL_LinearityCorrection(void)
{
    // 模拟多点校准数据
    float ref_points[] = {0.0f, 0.0001f, 0.0005f, 0.001f};
    float meas_points[] = {0.000002f, 0.000098f, 0.00049f, 0.00098f};
    int n = 4;
    
    // 计算线性拟合系数 (y = bx + c)
    float sum_x = 0, sum_y = 0, sum_xy = 0, sum_x2 = 0;
    for (int i = 0; i < n; i++) {
        sum_x += meas_points[i];
        sum_y += ref_points[i];
        sum_xy += meas_points[i] * ref_points[i];
        sum_x2 += meas_points[i] * meas_points[i];
    }
    
    float b = (n * sum_xy - sum_x * sum_y) / (n * sum_x2 - sum_x * sum_x);
    float c = (sum_y - b * sum_x) / n;
    
    // 验证拟合结果
    for (int i = 0; i < n; i++) {
        float corrected = b * meas_points[i] + c;
        float error = fabsf(corrected - ref_points[i]);
        float tolerance = ref_points[i] * 0.01f + 0.000001f;  // 1% + 1μA
        
        TEST_ASSERT(error < tolerance, "Linearity correction error too large");
    }
}

/*============================================================================
 * TC-CAL-005: CRC校验测试
 *============================================================================*/
void Test_CAL_CRC(void)
{
    CalibrationData_t test_data;
    memset(&test_data, 0x55, sizeof(test_data));
    
    // 计算CRC
    uint32_t crc1 = CAL_CalculateCRC(&test_data);
    
    // 再次计算，应该相同
    uint32_t crc2 = CAL_CalculateCRC(&test_data);
    TEST_ASSERT(crc1 == crc2, "CRC not repeatable");
    
    // 修改数据后CRC应该不同
    test_data.ranges[0].gain = 1.5f;
    uint32_t crc3 = CAL_CalculateCRC(&test_data);
    TEST_ASSERT(crc1 != crc3, "CRC not sensitive to data change");
}

/*============================================================================
 * TC-CAL-006: Flash存储测试
 *============================================================================*/
void Test_CAL_FlashStorage(void)
{
    // 保存当前校准数据
    const CalibrationData_t* original = CAL_GetData();
    CalibrationData_t backup;
    memcpy(&backup, original, sizeof(backup));
    
    // 修改数据
    CAL_ResetToFactory();
    
    // 保存到Flash
    bool save_ok = CAL_SaveToFlash();
    TEST_ASSERT(save_ok, "Flash save failed");
    
    // 从Flash加载
    bool load_ok = CAL_LoadFromFlash();
    TEST_ASSERT(load_ok, "Flash load failed");
    
    // 验证数据
    const CalibrationData_t* loaded = CAL_GetData();
    TEST_ASSERT(loaded->magic == CAL_MAGIC_NUMBER, "Loaded data corrupted");
}

/*============================================================================
 * 运行所有校准测试
 *============================================================================*/
void Test_Calibration_Suite(void)
{
    Test_Init("校准算法测试");
    
    Test_Run("TC-CAL-001", "校准数据初始化", Test_CAL_Init);
    Test_Run("TC-CAL-002", "偏移校准", Test_CAL_OffsetCorrection);
    Test_Run("TC-CAL-003", "增益校准", Test_CAL_GainCorrection);
    Test_Run("TC-CAL-004", "线性度校准", Test_CAL_LinearityCorrection);
    Test_Run("TC-CAL-005", "CRC校验", Test_CAL_CRC);
    Test_Run("TC-CAL-006", "Flash存储", Test_CAL_FlashStorage);
    
    Test_PrintReport();
}
```

## 四、集成测试

### 4.1 档位切换测试

```c
/*============================================================================
 * test_range_switching.c - 档位切换集成测试
 *============================================================================*/
#include "test_framework.h"
#include "current_meter.h"

/*============================================================================
 * 测试辅助函数: 设置测试电流
 * 需要外部可编程电流源
 *============================================================================*/
extern void TestFixture_SetCurrent(float current_a);
extern float TestFixture_GetActualCurrent(void);

/*============================================================================
 * TC-RSW-001: μA档基本功能测试
 *============================================================================*/
void Test_Range_UA_Basic(void)
{
    // 强制切换到μA档
    CM_ForceRange(RANGE_UA);
    Test_Delay(100);
    
    // 设置测试电流: 100μA
    TestFixture_SetCurrent(0.0001f);
    Test_Delay(500);
    
    // 读取测量值
    float measured = Test_GetStableCurrent(10, 10);
    float expected = TestFixture_GetActualCurrent();
    
    // 允许 1% 误差
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL(expected, measured, expected * 0.01f);
}

/*============================================================================
 * TC-RSW-002: mA档基本功能测试
 *============================================================================*/
void Test_Range_MA_Basic(void)
{
    CM_ForceRange(RANGE_MA);
    Test_Delay(100);
    
    // 设置测试电流: 10mA
    TestFixture_SetCurrent(0.01f);
    Test_Delay(500);
    
    float measured = Test_GetStableCurrent(10, 10);
    float expected = TestFixture_GetActualCurrent();
    
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL(expected, measured, expected * 0.01f);
}

/*============================================================================
 * TC-RSW-003: A档基本功能测试
 *============================================================================*/
void Test_Range_A_Basic(void)
{
    CM_ForceRange(RANGE_A);
    Test_Delay(100);
    
    // 设置测试电流: 1A
    TestFixture_SetCurrent(1.0f);
    Test_Delay(500);
    
    float measured = Test_GetStableCurrent(10, 10);
    float expected = TestFixture_GetActualCurrent();
    
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL(expected, measured, expected * 0.01f);
}

/*============================================================================
 * TC-RSW-004: 自动上切测试 (μA → mA)
 *============================================================================*/
void Test_AutoRange_UpSwitch_UA_to_MA(void)
{
    // 启用自动切档
    CM_SetAutoRange(true);
    
    // 从μA档开始
    CM_ForceRange(RANGE_UA);
    TestFixture_SetCurrent(0.0001f);  // 100μA
    Test_Delay(500);
    
    TEST_ASSERT(CM_GetCurrentRange() == RANGE_UA, "Should start in UA range");
    
    // 增加电流到 0.9mA (超过上切阈值 0.8mA)
    TestFixture_SetCurrent(0.0009f);
    Test_Delay(200);
    
    // 应该自动切换到mA档
    TEST_ASSERT(CM_GetCurrentRange() == RANGE_MA, "Should switch to MA range");
    
    // 验证测量值
    float measured = Test_GetStableCurrent(10, 10);
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL(0.0009f, measured, 0.0009f * 0.02f);
}

/*============================================================================
 * TC-RSW-005: 自动上切测试 (mA → A)
 *============================================================================*/
void Test_AutoRange_UpSwitch_MA_to_A(void)
{
    CM_SetAutoRange(true);
    CM_ForceRange(RANGE_MA);
    TestFixture_SetCurrent(0.01f);  // 10mA
    Test_Delay(500);
    
    TEST_ASSERT(CM_GetCurrentRange() == RANGE_MA, "Should start in MA range");
    
    // 增加电流到 90mA (超过上切阈值 80mA)
    TestFixture_SetCurrent(0.09f);
    Test_Delay(200);
    
    TEST_ASSERT(CM_GetCurrentRange() == RANGE_A, "Should switch to A range");
    
    float measured = Test_GetStableCurrent(10, 10);
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL(0.09f, measured, 0.09f * 0.02f);
}

/*============================================================================
 * TC-RSW-006: 自动下切测试 (mA → μA)
 *============================================================================*/
void Test_AutoRange_DownSwitch_MA_to_UA(void)
{
    CM_SetAutoRange(true);
    CM_ForceRange(RANGE_MA);
    TestFixture_SetCurrent(0.01f);  // 10mA
    Test_Delay(500);
    
    TEST_ASSERT(CM_GetCurrentRange() == RANGE_MA, "Should start in MA range");
    
    // 减小电流到 0.3mA (低于下切阈值 0.4mA)
    TestFixture_SetCurrent(0.0003f);
    Test_Delay(500);  // 下切需要更长时间确认
    
    TEST_ASSERT(CM_GetCurrentRange() == RANGE_UA, "Should switch to UA range");
    
    float measured = Test_GetStableCurrent(10, 10);
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL(0.0003f, measured, 0.0003f * 0.02f);
}

/*============================================================================
 * TC-RSW-007: 自动下切测试 (A → mA)
 *============================================================================*/
void Test_AutoRange_DownSwitch_A_to_MA(void)
{
    CM_SetAutoRange(true);
    CM_ForceRange(RANGE_A);
    TestFixture_SetCurrent(1.0f);  // 1A
    Test_Delay(500);
    
    TEST_ASSERT(CM_GetCurrentRange() == RANGE_A, "Should start in A range");
    
    // 减小电流到 30mA (低于下切阈值 40mA)
    TestFixture_SetCurrent(0.03f);
    Test_Delay(500);
    
    TEST_ASSERT(CM_GetCurrentRange() == RANGE_MA, "Should switch to MA range");
    
    float measured = Test_GetStableCurrent(10, 10);
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL(0.03f, measured, 0.03f * 0.02f);
}

/*============================================================================
 * TC-RSW-008: 迟滞区间测试 (不应切档)
 *============================================================================*/
void Test_AutoRange_Hysteresis(void)
{
    CM_SetAutoRange(true);
    
    // 在mA档，设置电流在迟滞区间内 (0.4mA ~ 0.8mA 对于μA/mA边界)
    CM_ForceRange(RANGE_MA);
    TestFixture_SetCurrent(0.0006f);  // 0.6mA，在迟滞区间内
    Test_Delay(1000);
    
    // 应该保持在mA档
    TEST_ASSERT(CM_GetCurrentRange() == RANGE_MA, "Should stay in MA range (hysteresis)");
    
    // 从μA档开始，同样的电流
    CM_ForceRange(RANGE_UA);
    TestFixture_SetCurrent(0.0006f);
    Test_Delay(1000);
    
    // 应该保持在μA档 (因为没超过上切阈值0.8mA)
    TEST_ASSERT(CM_GetCurrentRange() == RANGE_UA, "Should stay in UA range (hysteresis)");
}

/*============================================================================
 * TC-RSW-009: 快速电流变化测试
 *============================================================================*/
void Test_AutoRange_FastChange(void)
{
    CM_SetAutoRange(true);
    CM_ForceRange(RANGE_UA);
    
    // 快速从 100μA 跳变到 1A
    TestFixture_SetCurrent(0.0001f);
    Test_Delay(100);
    
    uint32_t start_time = HAL_GetTick();
    TestFixture_SetCurrent(1.0f);
    
    // 等待切档完成
    while (CM_GetCurrentRange() != RANGE_A && (HAL_GetTick() - start_time) < 1000) {
        CM_Process();
        HAL_Delay(1);
    }
    
    uint32_t switch_time = HAL_GetTick() - start_time;
    
    // 切档应该在 100ms 内完成
    TEST_ASSERT(switch_time < 100, "Range switch too slow");
    TEST_ASSERT(CM_GetCurrentRange() == RANGE_A, "Should be in A range");
    
    snprintf(g_current_test->message, sizeof(g_current_test->message),
             "Switch time: %lu ms", switch_time);
}

/*============================================================================
 * TC-RSW-010: 连续切档稳定性测试
 *============================================================================*/
void Test_AutoRange_Stability(void)
{
    CM_SetAutoRange(true);
    
    // 在切档边界附近波动，不应频繁切档
    float currents[] = {0.00075f, 0.00085f, 0.00078f, 0.00082f, 0.00079f};
    int switch_count = 0;
    CurrentRange_t last_range = CM_GetCurrentRange();
    
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; i++) {
        TestFixture_SetCurrent(currents[i]);
        Test_Delay(200);
        
        if (CM_GetCurrentRange() != last_range) {
            switch_count++;
            last_range = CM_GetCurrentRange();
        }
    }
    
    // 切档次数应该很少 (迟滞应该防止频繁切换)
    TEST_ASSERT(switch_count <= 2, "Too many range switches (unstable)");
    
    snprintf(g_current_test->message, sizeof(g_current_test->message),
             "Switch count: %d", switch_count);
}

/*============================================================================
 * 运行所有档位切换测试
 *============================================================================*/
void Test_RangeSwitching_Suite(void)
{
    Test_Init("档位切换测试");
    
    Test_Run("TC-RSW-001", "μA档基本功能", Test_Range_UA_Basic);
    Test_Run("TC-RSW-002", "mA档基本功能", Test_Range_MA_Basic);
    Test_Run("TC-RSW-003", "A档基本功能", Test_Range_A_Basic);
    Test_Run("TC-RSW-004", "自动上切 μA→mA", Test_AutoRange_UpSwitch_UA_to_MA);
    Test_Run("TC-RSW-005", "自动上切 mA→A", Test_AutoRange_UpSwitch_MA_to_A);
    Test_Run("TC-RSW-006", "自动下切 mA→μA", Test_AutoRange_DownSwitch_MA_to_UA);
    Test_Run("TC-RSW-007", "自动下切 A→mA", Test_AutoRange_DownSwitch_A_to_MA);
    Test_Run("TC-RSW-008", "迟滞区间", Test_AutoRange_Hysteresis);
    Test_Run("TC-RSW-009", "快速电流变化", Test_AutoRange_FastChange);
    Test_Run("TC-RSW-010", "连续切档稳定性", Test_AutoRange_Stability);
    
    Test_PrintReport();
}
```

## 五、精度测试

```c
/*============================================================================
 * test_accuracy.c - 精度测试
 *============================================================================*/
#include "test_framework.h"
#include "current_meter.h"

/*============================================================================
 * 精度测试点定义
 *============================================================================*/
typedef struct {
    float       test_current;       // 测试电流 (A)
    float       tolerance_percent;  // 允许误差 (%)
    float       tolerance_absolute; // 绝对误差 (A)
    const char* description;        // 描述
} AccuracyTestPoint_t;

// μA档测试点
static const AccuracyTestPoint_t g_ua_test_points[] = {
    {0.0000001f, 5.0f,  0.00000005f, "0.1μA"},
    {0.000001f,  2.0f,  0.00000002f, "1μA"},
    {0.00001f,   1.0f,  0.0000001f,  "10μA"},
    {0.0001f,    0.5f,  0.0000005f,  "100μA"},
    {0.0005f,    0.5f,  0.0000025f,  "500μA"},
    {0.0008f,    0.5f,  0.000004f,   "800μA"},
};

// mA档测试点
static const AccuracyTestPoint_t g_ma_test_points[] = {
    {0.001f,    1.0f,  0.00001f,   "1mA"},
    {0.005f,    0.5f,  0.000025f,  "5mA"},
    {0.01f,     0.5f,  0.00005f,   "10mA"},
    {0.05f,     0.5f,  0.00025f,   "50mA"},
    {0.08f,     0.5f,  0.0004f,    "80mA"},
};

// A档测试点
static const AccuracyTestPoint_t g_a_test_points[] = {
    {0.1f,      1.0f,  0.001f,     "100mA"},
    {0.5f,      0.5f,  0.0025f,    "500mA"},
    {1.0f,      0.5f,  0.005f,     "1A"},
    {2.0f,      0.5f,  0.01f,      "2A"},
    {4.0f,      0.5f,  0.02f,      "4A"},
    {5.0f,      1.0f,  0.05f,      "5A"},
};

/*============================================================================
 * TC-ACC-001: μA档精度测试
 *============================================================================*/
void Test_Accuracy_UA_Range(void)
{
    CM_SetAutoRange(false);
    CM_ForceRange(RANGE_UA);
    Test_Delay(100);
    
    int pass_count = 0;
    int total = sizeof(g_ua_test_points) / sizeof(g_ua_test_points[0]);
    
    printf("│ μA档精度测试:                                                │\n");
    
    for (int i = 0; i < total; i++) {
        const AccuracyTestPoint_t* pt = &g_ua_test_points[i];
        
        TestFixture_SetCurrent(pt->test_current);
        Test_Delay(500);
        
        float measured = Test_GetStableCurrent(20, 10);
        float expected = TestFixture_GetActualCurrent();
        float error = fabsf(measured - expected);
        float error_percent = (expected > 0) ? (error / expected * 100.0f) : 0;
        
        float tolerance = fmaxf(expected * pt->tolerance_percent / 100.0f, 
                                pt->tolerance_absolute);
        
        bool pass = (error <= tolerance);
        if (pass) pass_count++;
        
        printf("│   %8s: 期望=%.3e, 测量=%.3e, 误差=%.2f%% %s │\n",
               pt->description, expected, measured, error_percent,
               pass ? "✓" : "✗");
    }
    
    TEST_ASSERT(pass_count == total, "Some accuracy tests failed");
    
    snprintf(g_current_test->message, sizeof(g_current_test->message),
             "Passed %d/%d points", pass_count, total);
}

/*============================================================================
 * TC-ACC-002: mA档精度测试
 *============================================================================*/
void Test_Accuracy_MA_Range(void)
{
    CM_SetAutoRange(false);
    CM_ForceRange(RANGE_MA);
    Test_Delay(100);
    
    int pass_count = 0;
    int total = sizeof(g_ma_test_points) / sizeof(g_ma_test_points[0]);
    
    printf("│ mA档精度测试:                                                │\n");
    
    for (int i = 0; i < total; i++) {
        const AccuracyTestPoint_t* pt = &g_ma_test_points[i];
        
        TestFixture_SetCurrent(pt->test_current);
        Test_Delay(500);
        
        float measured = Test_GetStableCurrent(20, 10);
        float expected = TestFixture_GetActualCurrent();
        float error = fabsf(measured - expected);
        float error_percent = (expected > 0) ? (error / expected * 100.0f) : 0;
        
        float tolerance = fmaxf(expected * pt->tolerance_percent / 100.0f,
                                pt->tolerance_absolute);
        
        bool pass = (error <= tolerance);
        if (pass) pass_count++;
        
        printf("│   %8s: 期望=%.3e, 测量=%.3e, 误差=%.2f%% %s │\n",
               pt->description, expected, measured, error_percent,
               pass ? "✓" : "✗");
    }
    
    TEST_ASSERT(pass_count == total, "Some accuracy tests failed");
}

/*============================================================================
 * TC-ACC-003: A档精度测试
 *============================================================================*/
void Test_Accuracy_A_Range(void)
{
    CM_SetAutoRange(false);
    CM_ForceRange(RANGE_A);
    Test_Delay(100);
    
    int pass_count = 0;
    int total = sizeof(g_a_test_points) / sizeof(g_a_test_points[0]);
    
    printf("│ A档精度测试:                                                 │\n");
    
    for (int i = 0; i < total; i++) {
        const AccuracyTestPoint_t* pt = &g_a_test_points[i];
        
        TestFixture_SetCurrent(pt->test_current);
        Test_Delay(500);
        
        float measured = Test_GetStableCurrent(20, 10);
        float expected = TestFixture_GetActualCurrent();
        float error = fabsf(measured - expected);
        float error_percent = (expected > 0) ? (error / expected * 100.0f) : 0;
        
        float tolerance = fmaxf(expected * pt->tolerance_percent / 100.0f,
                                pt->tolerance_absolute);
        
        bool pass = (error <= tolerance);
        if (pass) pass_count++;
        
        printf("│   %8s: 期望=%.3e, 测量=%.3e, 误差=%.2f%% %s │\n",
               pt->description, expected, measured, error_percent,
               pass ? "✓" : "✗");
    }
    
    TEST_ASSERT(pass_count == total, "Some accuracy tests failed");
}

/*============================================================================
 * TC-ACC-004: 全量程自动切档精度测试
 *============================================================================*/
void Test_Accuracy_AutoRange_Full(void)
{
    CM_SetAutoRange(true);
    
    // 测试点覆盖全量程
    float test_currents[] = {
        0.000001f,   // 1μA
        0.00001f,    // 10μA
        0.0001f,     // 100μA
        0.001f,      // 1mA
        0.01f,       // 10mA
        0.1f,        // 100mA
        1.0f,        // 1A
        5.0f         // 5A
    };
    
    int total = sizeof(test_currents) / sizeof(test_currents[0]);
    int pass_count = 0;
    
    printf("│ 全量程自动切档精度测试:                                      │\n");
    
    for (int i = 0; i < total; i++) {
        TestFixture_SetCurrent(test_currents[i]);
        Test_Delay(1000);  // 等待切档稳定
        
        float measured = Test_GetStableCurrent(20, 10);
        float expected = TestFixture_GetActualCurrent();
        float error_percent = fabsf(measured - expected) / expected * 100.0f;
        
        // 全量程允许 2% 误差
        bool pass = (error_percent < 2.0f);
        if (pass) pass_count++;
        
        printf("│   %.2e A: 测量=%.3e, 误差=%.2f%%, 档位=%d %s │\n",
               test_currents[i], measured, error_percent,
               CM_GetCurrentRange(), pass ? "✓" : "✗");
    }
    
    TEST_ASSERT(pass_count == total, "Some auto-range accuracy tests failed");
}

/*============================================================================
 * TC-ACC-005: 零点漂移测试
 *============================================================================*/
void Test_Accuracy_ZeroDrift(void)
{
    CM_SetAutoRange(false);
    
    // 断开负载
    TestFixture_SetCurrent(0.0f);
    Test_Delay(1000);
    
    printf("│ 零点漂移测试 (10分钟):                                       │\n");
    
    float max_drift = 0;
    float initial_reading = 0;
    
    // 测试每个档位
    for (int range = RANGE_UA; range <= RANGE_A; range++) {
        CM_ForceRange(range);
        Test_Delay(500);
        
        initial_reading = Test_GetStableCurrent(10, 10);
        
        // 监测10分钟
        for (int minute = 0; minute < 10; minute++) {
            Test_Delay(60000);  // 1分钟
            
            float current_reading = Test_GetStableCurrent(10, 10);
            float drift = fabsf(current_reading - initial_reading);
            
            if (drift > max_drift) {
                max_drift = drift;
            }
            
            printf("│   档位%d, 第%d分钟: 漂移=%.3e A                          │\n",
                   range, minute + 1, drift);
        }
    }
    
    // 零点漂移应小于 1μA
    TEST_ASSERT(max_drift < 0.000001f, "Zero drift too large");
    
    snprintf(g_current_test->message, sizeof(g_current_test->message),
             "Max drift: %.3e A", max_drift);
}

/*============================================================================
 * TC-ACC-006: 噪声测试
 *============================================================================*/
void Test_Accuracy_Noise(void)
{
    CM_SetAutoRange(false);
    
    printf("│ 噪声测试:                                                    │\n");
    
    // 测试每个档位的噪声
    float test_currents[] = {0.0001f, 0.01f, 1.0f};  // 各档位典型值
    
    for (int range = RANGE_UA; range <= RANGE_A; range++) {
        CM_ForceRange(range);
        TestFixture_SetCurrent(test_currents[range]);
        Test_Delay(500);
        
        // 采集100个样本
        float samples[100];
        float sum = 0, sum_sq = 0;
        
        for (int i = 0; i < 100; i++) {
            CM_Process();
            samples[i] = CM_GetCurrent_A();
            sum += samples[i];
            HAL_Delay(10);
        }
        
        float mean = sum / 100.0f;
        
        for (int i = 0; i < 100; i++) {
            float diff = samples[i] - mean;
            sum_sq += diff * diff;
        }
        
        float std_dev = sqrtf(sum_sq / 100.0f);
        float noise_percent = (mean > 0) ? (std_dev / mean * 100.0f) : 0;
        
        printf("│   档位%d: 均值=%.3e, 标准差=%.3e, 噪声=%.3f%%           │\n",
               range, mean, std_dev, noise_percent);
        
        // 噪声应小于 0.1%
        TEST_ASSERT(noise_percent < 0.1f, "Noise too high");
    }
}

/*============================================================================
 * 运行所有精度测试
 *============================================================================*/
void Test_Accuracy_Suite(void)
{
    Test_Init("精度测试");
    
    Test_Run("TC-ACC-001", "μA档精度", Test_Accuracy_UA_Range);
    Test_Run("TC-ACC-002", "mA档精度", Test_Accuracy_MA_Range);
    Test_Run("TC-ACC-003", "A档精度", Test_Accuracy_A_Range);
    Test_Run("TC-ACC-004", "全量程自动切档精度", Test_Accuracy_AutoRange_Full);
    Test_Run("TC-ACC-005", "零点漂移", Test_Accuracy_ZeroDrift);
    Test_Run("TC-ACC-006", "噪声测试", Test_Accuracy_Noise);
    
    Test_PrintReport();
}
```

## 六、异常测试

```c
/*============================================================================
 * test_exception.c - 异常测试
 *============================================================================*/
#include "test_framework.h"
#include "current_meter.h"

/*============================================================================
 * TC-EXC-001: 过载保护测试
 *============================================================================*/
void Test_Exception_Overload(void)
{
    CM_SetAutoRange(true);
    CM_ForceRange(RANGE_UA);
    
    // 正常电流
    TestFixture_SetCurrent(0.0001f);
    Test_Delay(500);
    
    TEST_ASSERT(!CM_IsOverload(), "Should not be overload initially");
    
    // 施加过载电流 (6A，超过5A限制)
    TestFixture_SetCurrent(6.0f);
    Test_Delay(100);
    
    // 应该检测到过载
    TEST_ASSERT(CM_IsOverload(), "Should detect overload");
    TEST_ASSERT(CM_GetState() == CM_STATE_OVERLOAD, "Should be in overload state");
    
    // 恢复正常电流
    TestFixture_SetCurrent(1.0f);
    Test_Delay(500);
    
    // 应该恢复正常
    TEST_ASSERT(!CM_IsOverload(), "Should recover from overload");
}

/*============================================================================
 * TC-EXC-002: 硬件保护触发测试
 *============================================================================*/
void Test_Exception_HardwareProtect(void)
{
    CM_SetAutoRange(true);
    CM_ForceRange(RANGE_UA);
    
    // 记录初始状态
    bool hw_protect_before = CM_IsHardwareProtectActive();
    
    // 施加大电流触发硬件保护
    TestFixture_SetCurrent(2.0f);
    Test_Delay(10);  // 硬件保护应该很快触发
    
    // 检查硬件保护是否触发
    // 注意: 这取决于硬件比较器的配置
    bool hw_protect_after = CM_IsHardwareProtectActive();
    
    printf("│   硬件保护: 前=%d, 后=%d                                     │\n",
           hw_protect_before, hw_protect_after);
    
    // 恢复
    TestFixture_SetCurrent(0.0f);
    Test_Delay(500);
}

/*============================================================================
 * TC-EXC-003: 设备断开检测测试
 *============================================================================*/
void Test_Exception_Disconnect(void)
{
    CM_SetAutoRange(true);
    
    // 正常电流
    TestFixture_SetCurrent(0.001f);
    Test_Delay(500);
    
    TEST_ASSERT(!CM_IsDisconnected(), "Should not be disconnected initially");
    
    // 断开负载 (电流降到接近0)
    TestFixture_SetCurrent(0.0f);
    Test_Delay(1000);  // 等待断开检测
    
    // 应该检测到断开
    TEST_ASSERT(CM_IsDisconnected(), "Should detect disconnect");
    
    // 重新连接
    TestFixture_SetCurrent(0.001f);
    Test_Delay(500);
    
    // 应该检测到重新连接
    TEST_ASSERT(!CM_IsDisconnected(), "Should detect reconnect");
}

/*============================================================================
 * TC-EXC-004: 电流突变测试 (上升沿)
 *============================================================================*/
void Test_Exception_CurrentSurge_Rising(void)
{
    CM_SetAutoRange(true);
    CM_ForceRange(RANGE_UA);
    
    // 初始小电流
    TestFixture_SetCurrent(0.0001f);
    Test_Delay(500);
    
    // 记录切档前状态
    CurrentRange_t range_before = CM_GetCurrentRange();
    uint32_t start_time = HAL_GetTick();
    
    // 突然增加到大电流
    TestFixture_SetCurrent(3.0f);
    
    // 等待系统响应
    while ((HAL_GetTick() - start_time) < 500) {
        CM_Process();
        HAL_Delay(1);
    }
    
    // 检查是否正确切换到A档
    TEST_ASSERT(CM_GetCurrentRange() == RANGE_A, "Should switch to A range");
    
    // 检查测量值是否正确
    float measured = Test_GetStableCurrent(10, 10);
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL(3.0f, measured, 3.0f * 0.05f);  // 允许5%误差
    
    uint32_t response_time = HAL_GetTick() - start_time;
    snprintf(g_current_test->message, sizeof(g_current_test->message),
             "Response time: %lu ms", response_time);
}

/*============================================================================
 * TC-EXC-005: 电流突变测试 (下降沿)
 *============================================================================*/
void Test_Exception_CurrentSurge_Falling(void)
{
    CM_SetAutoRange(true);
    
    // 初始大电流
    TestFixture_SetCurrent(2.0f);
    Test_Delay(500);
    
    TEST_ASSERT(CM_GetCurrentRange() == RANGE_A, "Should be in A range");
    
    uint32_t start_time = HAL_GetTick();
    
    // 突然降到小电流
    TestFixture_SetCurrent(0.0001f);
    
    // 等待系统响应
    while (CM_GetCurrentRange() != RANGE_UA && (HAL_GetTick() - start_time) < 2000) {
        CM_Process();
        HAL_Delay(1);
    }
    
    // 检查是否正确切换到μA档
    TEST_ASSERT(CM_GetCurrentRange() == RANGE_UA, "Should switch to UA range");
    
    // 检查测量值
    float measured = Test_GetStableCurrent(10, 10);
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL(0.0001f, measured, 0.0001f * 0.05f);
    
    uint32_t response_time = HAL_GetTick() - start_time;
    snprintf(g_current_test->message, sizeof(g_current_test->message),
             "Response time: %lu ms", response_time);
}

    float reverse = CM_GetCurrent_A();
```

```c
/*============================================================================
 * TC-EXC-007: SPI通信故障恢复测试
 *============================================================================*/
void Test_Exception_SPIFault(void)
{
    // 模拟SPI通信故障
    // 注意: 这需要特殊的测试夹具或软件模拟
    
    // 正常读取
    float current1 = CM_GetCurrent_A();
    TEST_ASSERT(!isnan(current1), "Initial reading should be valid");
    
    // 模拟通信故障 (如果有故障注入接口)
    // TestFixture_InjectSPIFault(true);
    // Test_Delay(100);
    // TestFixture_InjectSPIFault(false);
    
    // 等待恢复
    Test_Delay(500);
    
    // 应该能恢复正常读取
    float current2 = CM_GetCurrent_A();
    TEST_ASSERT(!isnan(current2), "Should recover from SPI fault");
    
    snprintf(g_current_test->message, sizeof(g_current_test->message),
             "SPI fault recovery test (simulated)");
}

/*============================================================================
 * TC-EXC-008: 电源波动测试
 *============================================================================*/
void Test_Exception_PowerFluctuation(void)
{
    CM_SetAutoRange(false);
    CM_ForceRange(RANGE_MA);
    
    // 设置稳定电流
    TestFixture_SetCurrent(0.01f);
    Test_Delay(500);
    
    float baseline = Test_GetStableCurrent(20, 10);
    
    // 模拟电源波动 (如果测试夹具支持)
    // TestFixture_SetSupplyVoltage(3.0f);  // 降低电源电压
    // Test_Delay(100);
    // TestFixture_SetSupplyVoltage(3.3f);  // 恢复
    
    Test_Delay(500);
    
    float after_fluctuation = Test_GetStableCurrent(20, 10);
    
    // 电源波动后测量值应该稳定
    float error = fabsf(after_fluctuation - baseline) / baseline * 100.0f;
    TEST_ASSERT(error < 1.0f, "Measurement unstable after power fluctuation");
    
    snprintf(g_current_test->message, sizeof(g_current_test->message),
             "Error after fluctuation: %.2f%%", error);
}

/*============================================================================
 * TC-EXC-009: 极端温度响应测试
 *============================================================================*/
void Test_Exception_TemperatureExtreme(void)
{
    // 读取当前温度
    float temp = CM_GetTemperature();
    
    printf("│   当前芯片温度: %.2f °C                                      │\n", temp);
    
    // 检查温度是否在正常范围
    TEST_ASSERT_RANGE(temp, -10.0f, 70.0f);
    
    // 如果温度过高，应该有警告
    if (temp > 60.0f) {
        printf("│   警告: 温度偏高，可能影响精度                              │\n");
    }
    
    // 测试温度补偿是否工作
    CM_SetAutoRange(false);
    CM_ForceRange(RANGE_MA);
    TestFixture_SetCurrent(0.01f);
    Test_Delay(500);
    
    float current = Test_GetStableCurrent(20, 10);
    float expected = TestFixture_GetActualCurrent();
    float error = fabsf(current - expected) / expected * 100.0f;
    
    printf("│   温度 %.1f°C 时误差: %.2f%%                                  │\n", temp, error);
    
    // 即使在极端温度下，误差也应该在可接受范围内
    TEST_ASSERT(error < 2.0f, "Temperature compensation not working");
}

/*============================================================================
 * TC-EXC-010: 长时间过载恢复测试
 *============================================================================*/
void Test_Exception_ProlongedOverload(void)
{
    CM_SetAutoRange(true);
    
    // 正常电流
    TestFixture_SetCurrent(1.0f);
    Test_Delay(500);
    
    float baseline = Test_GetStableCurrent(10, 10);
    
    // 施加过载电流持续5秒
    TestFixture_SetCurrent(5.5f);
    Test_Delay(5000);
    
    // 恢复正常电流
    TestFixture_SetCurrent(1.0f);
    Test_Delay(1000);
    
    // 检查是否完全恢复
    float after_overload = Test_GetStableCurrent(10, 10);
    float drift = fabsf(after_overload - baseline) / baseline * 100.0f;
    
    printf("│   过载前: %.6f A                                            │\n", baseline);
    printf("│   过载后: %.6f A                                            │\n", after_overload);
    printf("│   漂移: %.2f%%                                               │\n", drift);
    
    // 过载恢复后漂移应小于1%
    TEST_ASSERT(drift < 1.0f, "Drift after prolonged overload too large");
}

/*============================================================================
 * 运行所有异常测试
 *============================================================================*/
void Test_Exception_Suite(void)
{
    Test_Init("异常测试");
    
    Test_Run("TC-EXC-001", "过载保护", Test_Exception_Overload);
    Test_Run("TC-EXC-002", "硬件保护触发", Test_Exception_HardwareProtect);
    Test_Run("TC-EXC-003", "设备断开检测", Test_Exception_Disconnect);
    Test_Run("TC-EXC-004", "电流突变(上升)", Test_Exception_CurrentSurge_Rising);
    Test_Run("TC-EXC-005", "电流突变(下降)", Test_Exception_CurrentSurge_Falling);
    Test_Run("TC-EXC-006", "反向电流", Test_Exception_ReverseCurrent);
    Test_Run("TC-EXC-007", "SPI通信故障恢复", Test_Exception_SPIFault);
    Test_Run("TC-EXC-008", "电源波动", Test_Exception_PowerFluctuation);
    Test_Run("TC-EXC-009", "极端温度响应", Test_Exception_TemperatureExtreme);
    Test_Run("TC-EXC-010", "长时间过载恢复", Test_Exception_ProlongedOverload);
    
    Test_PrintReport();
}
```

## 七、边界测试

```c
/*============================================================================
 * test_boundary.c - 边界测试
 *============================================================================*/
#include "test_framework.h"
#include "current_meter.h"

/*============================================================================
 * TC-BND-001: 最小可测电流测试
 *============================================================================*/
void Test_Boundary_MinCurrent(void)
{
    CM_SetAutoRange(false);
    CM_ForceRange(RANGE_UA);
    Test_Delay(100);
    
    // 测试最小电流 0.1μA
    float min_current = 0.0000001f;
    TestFixture_SetCurrent(min_current);
    Test_Delay(1000);
    
    float measured = Test_GetStableCurrent(50, 20);
    float expected = TestFixture_GetActualCurrent();
    
    printf("│   最小电流测试: 设定=%.2e A, 测量=%.2e A                    │\n",
           min_current, measured);
    
    // 最小电流允许较大误差 (10%)
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL(expected, measured, expected * 0.1f + 0.00000005f);
}

/*============================================================================
 * TC-BND-002: 最大可测电流测试
 *============================================================================*/
void Test_Boundary_MaxCurrent(void)
{
    CM_SetAutoRange(false);
    CM_ForceRange(RANGE_A);
    Test_Delay(100);
    
    // 测试最大电流 5A
    float max_current = 5.0f;
    TestFixture_SetCurrent(max_current);
    Test_Delay(1000);
    
    float measured = Test_GetStableCurrent(20, 10);
    float expected = TestFixture_GetActualCurrent();
    
    printf("│   最大电流测试: 设定=%.2f A, 测量=%.2f A                     │\n",
           max_current, measured);
    
    // 最大电流允许 1% 误差
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL(expected, measured, expected * 0.01f);
    
    // 检查是否接近过载
    TEST_ASSERT(!CM_IsOverload(), "Should not trigger overload at max current");
}

/*============================================================================
 * TC-BND-003: μA档上边界测试
 *============================================================================*/
void Test_Boundary_UA_Upper(void)
{
    CM_SetAutoRange(false);
    CM_ForceRange(RANGE_UA);
    Test_Delay(100);
    
    // μA档上边界: 1mA
    float boundary = 0.001f;
    TestFixture_SetCurrent(boundary);
    Test_Delay(500);
    
    float measured = Test_GetStableCurrent(20, 10);
    
    printf("│   μA档上边界: 设定=%.3f mA, 测量=%.3f mA                    │\n",
           boundary * 1000, measured * 1000);
    
    // 在边界处精度可能下降，允许 2% 误差
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL(boundary, measured, boundary * 0.02f);
}

/*============================================================================
 * TC-BND-004: μA档下边界测试
 *============================================================================*/
void Test_Boundary_UA_Lower(void)
{
    CM_SetAutoRange(false);
    CM_ForceRange(RANGE_UA);
    Test_Delay(100);
    
    // μA档下边界: 0.1μA
    float boundary = 0.0000001f;
    TestFixture_SetCurrent(boundary);
    Test_Delay(1000);
    
    float measured = Test_GetStableCurrent(50, 20);
    
    printf("│   μA档下边界: 设定=%.2f μA, 测量=%.2f μA                    │\n",
           boundary * 1e6, measured * 1e6);
    
    // 最小电流允许较大误差
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL(boundary, measured, boundary * 0.2f + 0.00000005f);
}

/*============================================================================
 * TC-BND-005: mA档边界测试
 *============================================================================*/
void Test_Boundary_MA_Range(void)
{
    CM_SetAutoRange(false);
    CM_ForceRange(RANGE_MA);
    Test_Delay(100);
    
    // mA档下边界: ~1μA (实际最小可靠值)
    float lower = 0.000001f;
    TestFixture_SetCurrent(lower);
    Test_Delay(500);
    float meas_lower = Test_GetStableCurrent(20, 10);
    
    // mA档上边界: 100mA
    float upper = 0.1f;
    TestFixture_SetCurrent(upper);
    Test_Delay(500);
    float meas_upper = Test_GetStableCurrent(20, 10);
    
    printf("│   mA档下边界: 设定=%.2f μA, 测量=%.2f μA                    │\n",
           lower * 1e6, meas_lower * 1e6);
    printf("│   mA档上边界: 设定=%.1f mA, 测量=%.1f mA                    │\n",
           upper * 1000, meas_upper * 1000);
    
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL(upper, meas_upper, upper * 0.02f);
}

/*============================================================================
 * TC-BND-006: A档边界测试
 *============================================================================*/
void Test_Boundary_A_Range(void)
{
    CM_SetAutoRange(false);
    CM_ForceRange(RANGE_A);
    Test_Delay(100);
    
    // A档下边界: ~30μA (实际最小可靠值)
    float lower = 0.00003f;
    TestFixture_SetCurrent(lower);
    Test_Delay(500);
    float meas_lower = Test_GetStableCurrent(20, 10);
    
    // A档上边界: 5A
    float upper = 5.0f;
    TestFixture_SetCurrent(upper);
    Test_Delay(500);
    float meas_upper = Test_GetStableCurrent(20, 10);
    
    printf("│   A档下边界: 设定=%.1f μA, 测量=%.1f μA                     │\n",
           lower * 1e6, meas_lower * 1e6);
    printf("│   A档上边界: 设定=%.1f A, 测量=%.1f A                       │\n",
           upper, meas_upper);
    
    TEST_ASSERT_EQUAL(upper, meas_upper, upper * 0.02f);
}

/*============================================================================
 * TC-BND-007: 切档阈值精确测试 (μA→mA)
 *============================================================================*/
void Test_Boundary_SwitchThreshold_UA_MA(void)
{
    CM_SetAutoRange(true);
    CM_ForceRange(RANGE_UA);
    Test_Delay(100);
    
    // 上切阈值: 0.8mA
    float threshold = 0.0008f;
    
    // 测试阈值以下 (不应切档)
    TestFixture_SetCurrent(threshold * 0.95f);
    Test_Delay(500);
    TEST_ASSERT(CM_GetCurrentRange() == RANGE_UA, "Should stay in UA below threshold");
    
    // 测试阈值以上 (应该切档)
    TestFixture_SetCurrent(threshold * 1.05f);
    Test_Delay(500);
    TEST_ASSERT(CM_GetCurrentRange() == RANGE_MA, "Should switch to MA above threshold");
    
    printf("│   μA→mA切档阈值测试通过 (阈值=%.2f mA)                      │\n",
           threshold * 1000);
}

/*============================================================================
 * TC-BND-008: 切档阈值精确测试 (mA→μA)
 *============================================================================*/
void Test_Boundary_SwitchThreshold_MA_UA(void)
{
    CM_SetAutoRange(true);
    CM_ForceRange(RANGE_MA);
    Test_Delay(100);
    
    // 下切阈值: 0.4mA
    float threshold = 0.0004f;
    
    // 测试阈值以上 (不应切档)
    TestFixture_SetCurrent(threshold * 1.2f);
    Test_Delay(500);
    TEST_ASSERT(CM_GetCurrentRange() == RANGE_MA, "Should stay in MA above threshold");
    
    // 测试阈值以下 (应该切档)
    TestFixture_SetCurrent(threshold * 0.8f);
    Test_Delay(1000);  // 下切需要更长确认时间
    TEST_ASSERT(CM_GetCurrentRange() == RANGE_UA, "Should switch to UA below threshold");
    
    printf("│   mA→μA切档阈值测试通过 (阈值=%.2f mA)                      │\n",
           threshold * 1000);
}

/*============================================================================
 * TC-BND-009: 切档阈值精确测试 (mA→A)
 *============================================================================*/
void Test_Boundary_SwitchThreshold_MA_A(void)
{
    CM_SetAutoRange(true);
    CM_ForceRange(RANGE_MA);
    Test_Delay(100);
    
    // 上切阈值: 80mA
    float threshold = 0.08f;
    
    TestFixture_SetCurrent(threshold * 0.95f);
    Test_Delay(500);
    TEST_ASSERT(CM_GetCurrentRange() == RANGE_MA, "Should stay in MA below threshold");
    
    TestFixture_SetCurrent(threshold * 1.05f);
    Test_Delay(500);
    TEST_ASSERT(CM_GetCurrentRange() == RANGE_A, "Should switch to A above threshold");
    
    printf("│   mA→A切档阈值测试通过 (阈值=%.1f mA)                       │\n",
           threshold * 1000);
}

/*============================================================================
 * TC-BND-010: 切档阈值精确测试 (A→mA)
 *============================================================================*/
void Test_Boundary_SwitchThreshold_A_MA(void)
{
    CM_SetAutoRange(true);
    CM_ForceRange(RANGE_A);
    Test_Delay(100);
    
    // 下切阈值: 40mA
    float threshold = 0.04f;
    
    TestFixture_SetCurrent(threshold * 1.2f);
    Test_Delay(500);
    TEST_ASSERT(CM_GetCurrentRange() == RANGE_A, "Should stay in A above threshold");
    
    TestFixture_SetCurrent(threshold * 0.8f);
    Test_Delay(1000);
    TEST_ASSERT(CM_GetCurrentRange() == RANGE_MA, "Should switch to MA below threshold");
    
    printf("│   A→mA切档阈值测试通过 (阈值=%.1f mA)                       │\n",
           threshold * 1000);
}

/*============================================================================
 * TC-BND-011: 电压测量边界测试
 *============================================================================*/
void Test_Boundary_Voltage(void)
{
    // 测试最小电压 (3V)
    TestFixture_SetVoltage(3.0f);
    Test_Delay(500);
    float v_min = CM_GetVoltage_V();
    
    // 测试最大电压 (24V)
    TestFixture_SetVoltage(24.0f);
    Test_Delay(500);
    float v_max = CM_GetVoltage_V();
    
    printf("│   电压范围测试: 最小=%.2f V, 最大=%.2f V                    │\n",
           v_min, v_max);
    
    TEST_ASSERT_RANGE(v_min, 2.8f, 3.2f);
    TEST_ASSERT_RANGE(v_max, 23.5f, 24.5f);
}

/*============================================================================
 * TC-BND-012: 分辨率验证测试
 *============================================================================*/
void Test_Boundary_Resolution(void)
{
    CM_SetAutoRange(false);
    
    printf("│   分辨率验证测试:                                            │\n");
    
    // μA档分辨率测试: 应能分辨 0.1μA
    CM_ForceRange(RANGE_UA);
    Test_Delay(100);
    
    TestFixture_SetCurrent(0.0000010f);  // 1.0μA
    Test_Delay(500);
    float reading1 = Test_GetStableCurrent(50, 10);
    
    TestFixture_SetCurrent(0.0000011f);  // 1.1μA (差0.1μA)
    Test_Delay(500);
    float reading2 = Test_GetStableCurrent(50, 10);
    
    float diff_ua = fabsf(reading2 - reading1);
    printf("│   μA档: 1.0μA vs 1.1μA, 差值=%.3f μA                        │\n",
           diff_ua * 1e6);
    
    // 应该能检测到 0.1μA 的差异
    TEST_ASSERT(diff_ua > 0.00000005f, "UA range resolution insufficient");
    
    // mA档分辨率测试: 应能分辨 1μA
    CM_ForceRange(RANGE_MA);
    Test_Delay(100);
    
    TestFixture_SetCurrent(0.001f);    // 1.000mA
    Test_Delay(500);
    reading1 = Test_GetStableCurrent(50, 10);
    
    TestFixture_SetCurrent(0.001001f); // 1.001mA (差1μA)
    Test_Delay(500);
    reading2 = Test_GetStableCurrent(50, 10);
    
    float diff_ma = fabsf(reading2 - reading1);
    printf("│   mA档: 1.000mA vs 1.001mA, 差值=%.3f μA                    │\n",
           diff_ma * 1e6);
    
    // A档分辨率测试: 应能分辨 100μA
    CM_ForceRange(RANGE_A);
    Test_Delay(100);
    
    TestFixture_SetCurrent(1.0f);      // 1.0000A
    Test_Delay(500);
    reading1 = Test_GetStableCurrent(50, 10);
    
    TestFixture_SetCurrent(1.0001f);   // 1.0001A (差100μA)
    Test_Delay(500);
    reading2 = Test_GetStableCurrent(50, 10);
    
    float diff_a = fabsf(reading2 - reading1);
    printf("│   A档: 1.0000A vs 1.0001A, 差值=%.1f μA                     │\n",
           diff_a * 1e6);
}

/*============================================================================
 * 运行所有边界测试
 *============================================================================*/
void Test_Boundary_Suite(void)
{
    Test_Init("边界测试");
    
    Test_Run("TC-BND-001", "最小可测电流", Test_Boundary_MinCurrent);
    Test_Run("TC-BND-002", "最大可测电流", Test_Boundary_MaxCurrent);
    Test_Run("TC-BND-003", "μA档上边界", Test_Boundary_UA_Upper);
    Test_Run("TC-BND-004", "μA档下边界", Test_Boundary_UA_Lower);
    Test_Run("TC-BND-005", "mA档边界", Test_Boundary_MA_Range);
    Test_Run("TC-BND-006", "A档边界", Test_Boundary_A_Range);
    Test_Run("TC-BND-007", "切档阈值μA→mA", Test_Boundary_SwitchThreshold_UA_MA);
    Test_Run("TC-BND-008", "切档阈值mA→μA", Test_Boundary_SwitchThreshold_MA_UA);
    Test_Run("TC-BND-009", "切档阈值mA→A", Test_Boundary_SwitchThreshold_MA_A);
    Test_Run("TC-BND-010", "切档阈值A→mA", Test_Boundary_SwitchThreshold_A_MA);
    Test_Run("TC-BND-011", "电压测量边界", Test_Boundary_Voltage);
    Test_Run("TC-BND-012", "分辨率验证", Test_Boundary_Resolution);
    
    Test_PrintReport();
}
```

## 八、长期稳定性测试

```c
/*============================================================================
 * test_stability.c - 长期稳定性测试
 *============================================================================*/
#include "test_framework.h"
#include "current_meter.h"

/*============================================================================
 * TC-STB-001: 24小时稳定性测试
 *============================================================================*/
void Test_Stability_24Hour(void)
{
    CM_SetAutoRange(false);
    CM_ForceRange(RANGE_MA);
    
    // 设置稳定电流
    TestFixture_SetCurrent(0.01f);  // 10mA
    Test_Delay(1000);
    
    float initial = Test_GetStableCurrent(100, 10);
    float min_val = initial;
    float max_val = initial;
    float sum = 0;
    int samples = 0;
    
    printf("│   24小时稳定性测试开始                                       │\n");
    printf("│   初始值: %.6f mA                                           │\n", initial * 1000);
    
    // 每小时记录一次
    for (int hour = 0; hour < 24; hour++) {
        // 等待1小时 (实际测试中)
        // 这里用短时间模拟
        Test_Delay(1000);  // 实际应为 3600000
        
        float current = Test_GetStableCurrent(100, 10);
        sum += current;
        samples++;
        
        if (current < min_val) min_val = current;
        if (current > max_val) max_val = current;
        
        float drift = (current - initial) / initial * 100.0f;
        
        printf("│   第%2d小时: %.6f mA, 漂移=%.3f%%                         │\n",
               hour + 1, current * 1000, drift);
    }
    
    float avg = sum / samples;
    float range = max_val - min_val;
    float range_percent = range / avg * 100.0f;
    
    printf("│   ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────│\n");
    printf("│   平均值: %.6f mA                                          │\n", avg * 1000);
    printf("│   最小值: %.6f mA                                          │\n", min_val * 1000);
    printf("│   最大值: %.6f mA                                          │\n", max_val * 1000);
    printf("│   波动范围: %.3f%% (%.3f μA)                                │\n", 
           range_percent, range * 1e6);
    
    // 24小时漂移应小于 0.1%
    TEST_ASSERT(range_percent < 0.1f, "24-hour drift too large");
}

/*============================================================================
 * TC-STB-002: 温度循环稳定性测试
 *============================================================================*/
void Test_Stability_TempCycle(void)
{
    CM_SetAutoRange(false);
    CM_ForceRange(RANGE_MA);
    TestFixture_SetCurrent(0.01f);
    
    printf("│   温度循环测试 (需要温箱)                                    │\n");
    
    // 温度点: -10°C, 25°C, 50°C
    float temps[] = {-10.0f, 25.0f, 50.0f};
    float readings[3];
    
    for (int i = 0; i < 3; i++) {
        // 设置温箱温度 (如果有)
        // TestFixture_SetChamberTemp(temps[i]);
        // Test_Delay(1800000);  // 等待30分钟稳定
        
        Test_Delay(1000);  // 模拟
        
        readings[i] = Test_GetStableCurrent(50, 10);
        float chip_temp = CM_GetTemperature();
        
        printf("│   环境温度 %.0f°C: 芯片温度=%.1f°C, 电流=%.6f mA          │\n",
               temps[i], chip_temp, readings[i] * 1000);
    }
    
    // 计算温度系数
    float temp_range = temps[2] - temps[0];
    float current_range = readings[2] - readings[0];
    float temp_coeff = current_range / readings[1] / temp_range * 1e6;  // ppm/°C
    
    printf("│   温度系数: %.1f ppm/°C                                     │\n", temp_coeff);
    
    // 温度系数应小于 50 ppm/°C
    TEST_ASSERT(fabsf(temp_coeff) < 50.0f, "Temperature coefficient too large");
}

/*============================================================================
 * TC-STB-003: 连续切档稳定性测试
 *============================================================================*/
void Test_Stability_RangeSwitching(void)
{
    CM_SetAutoRange(true);
    
    printf("│   连续切档稳定性测试 (1000次循环)                            │\n");
    
    float currents[] = {0.0001f, 0.001f, 0.01f, 0.1f, 1.0f};
    int num_currents = sizeof(currents) / sizeof(currents[0]);
    
    int error_count = 0;
    
    for (int cycle = 0; cycle < 1000; cycle++) {
        for (int i = 0; i < num_currents; i++) {
            TestFixture_SetCurrent(currents[i]);
            Test_Delay(50);
            
            float measured = CM_GetCurrent_A();
            float error = fabsf(measured - currents[i]) / currents[i];
            
            if (error > 0.05f) {  // 5% 误差阈值
                error_count++;
            }
        }
        
        if ((cycle + 1) % 100 == 0) {
            printf("│   完成 %d 次循环, 错误次数: %d                             │\n",
                   cycle + 1, error_count);
        }
    }
    
    float error_rate = (float)error_count / (1000 * num_currents) * 100.0f;
    printf("│   总错误率: %.2f%%                                           │\n", error_rate);
    
    // 错误率应小于 0.1%
    TEST_ASSERT(error_rate < 0.1f, "Range switching error rate too high");
}

/*============================================================================
 * TC-STB-004: 电源开关循环测试
 *============================================================================*/
void Test_Stability_PowerCycle(void)
{
    printf("│   电源开关循环测试 (100次)                                   │\n");
    
    float reference_current = 0.01f;
    TestFixture_SetCurrent(reference_current);
    
    float initial = Test_GetStableCurrent(20, 10);
    int fail_count = 0;
    
    for (int cycle = 0; cycle < 100; cycle++) {
        // 模拟电源关闭
        // TestFixture_PowerOff();
        // Test_Delay(1000);
        
        // 模拟电源开启
        // TestFixture_PowerOn();
        CM_Init();
        Test_Delay(500);
        
        float current = Test_GetStableCurrent(10, 10);
        float error = fabsf(current - initial) / initial * 100.0f;
        
        if (error > 1.0f) {
            fail_count++;
            printf("│   循环 %d: 误差 %.2f%% (失败)                              │\n",
                   cycle + 1, error);
        }
    }
    
    printf("│   电源循环失败次数: %d/100                                   │\n", fail_count);
    
    TEST_ASSERT(fail_count == 0, "Power cycle stability failed");
}

/*============================================================================
 * TC-STB-005: 数据记录完整性测试
 *============================================================================*/
void Test_Stability_DataIntegrity(void)
{
    CM_SetAutoRange(true);
    
    printf("│   数据记录完整性测试 (10000个样本)                           │\n");
    
    TestFixture_SetCurrent(0.01f);
    Test_Delay(500);
    
    int invalid_count = 0;
    int nan_count = 0;
    int range_error_count = 0;
    float prev_current = 0;
    
    for (int i = 0; i < 10000; i++) {
        CM_Process();
        
        float current = CM_GetCurrent_A();
        float voltage = CM_GetVoltage_V();
        
        // 检查 NaN
        if (isnan(current) || isnan(voltage)) {
            nan_count++;
            continue;
        }
        
        // 检查范围
        if (current < 0 || current > 10.0f) {
            range_error_count++;
        }
        
        // 检查突变 (不应该有超过10倍的突变)
        if (prev_current > 0 && fabsf(current - prev_current) > prev_current * 10) {
            invalid_count++;
        }
        
        prev_current = current;
        HAL_Delay(1);
    }
    
    printf("│   NaN 数量: %d                                               │\n", nan_count);
    printf("│   范围错误: %d                                               │\n", range_error_count);
    printf("│   无效数据: %d                                               │\n", invalid_count);
    
    int total_errors = nan_count + range_error_count + invalid_count;
    TEST_ASSERT(total_errors == 0, "Data integrity errors detected");
}

/*============================================================================
 * 运行所有稳定性测试
 *============================================================================*/
void Test_Stability_Suite(void)
{
    Test_Init("稳定性测试");
    
    Test_Run("TC-STB-001", "24小时稳定性", Test_Stability_24Hour);
    Test_Run("TC-STB-002", "温度循环稳定性", Test_Stability_TempCycle);
    Test_Run("TC-STB-003", "连续切档稳定性", Test_Stability_RangeSwitching);
    Test_Run("TC-STB-004", "电源开关循环", Test_Stability_PowerCycle);
    Test_Run("TC-STB-005", "数据记录完整性", Test_Stability_DataIntegrity);
    
    Test_PrintReport();
}
```

## 九、测试主程序

```c
/*============================================================================
 * test_main.c - 测试主程序
 *============================================================================*/
#include "test_framework.h"
#include "current_meter.h"

/*============================================================================
 * 测试套件声明
 *============================================================================*/
extern void Test_INA229_Driver_Suite(void);
extern void Test_Calibration_Suite(void);
extern void Test_RangeSwitching_Suite(void);
extern void Test_Accuracy_Suite(void);
extern void Test_Exception_Suite(void);
extern void Test_Boundary_Suite(void);
extern void Test_Stability_Suite(void);

/*============================================================================
 * 测试菜单
 *============================================================================*/
void Test_ShowMenu(void)
{
    printf("\n");
    printf("╔══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╗\n");
    printf("║                    电流计测试系统                            ║\n");
    printf("╠══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╣\n");
    printf("║  1. INA229 驱动测试                                          ║\n");
    printf("║  2. 校准算法测试                                             ║\n");
    printf("║  3. 档位切换测试                                             ║\n");
    printf("║  4. 精度测试                                                 ║\n");
    printf("║  5. 异常测试                                                 ║\n");
    printf("║  6. 边界测试                                                 ║\n");
    printf("║  7. 稳定性测试                                               ║\n");
    printf("║  8. 运行所有测试                                             ║\n");
    printf("║  9. 快速测试 (基本功能验证)                                  ║\n");
    printf("║  0. 退出                                                     ║\n");
    printf("╚══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╝\n");
    printf("请选择: ");
}

/*============================================================================
 * 快速测试 (生产线用)
 *============================================================================*/
void Test_QuickTest(void)
{
    Test_Init("快速测试 (生产验证)");
    
    printf("│ 快速测试包含基本功能验证，用于生产线测试                     │\n");
    printf("│ 预计耗时: 2-3 分钟                                           │\n\n");
    
    // 1. 设备ID检查
    Test_Run("QT-001", "设备ID检查", Test_INA229_DeviceID);
    
    // 2. 各档位基本功能
    Test_Run("QT-002", "μA档基本功能", Test_Range_UA_Basic);
    Test_Run("QT-003", "mA档基本功能", Test_Range_MA_Basic);
    Test_Run("QT-004", "A档基本功能", Test_Range_A_Basic);
    
    // 3. 自动切档
    Test_Run("QT-005", "自动切档 μA→mA", Test_AutoRange_UpSwitch_UA_to_MA);
    Test_Run("QT-006", "自动切档 mA→A", Test_AutoRange_UpSwitch_MA_to_A);
    
    // 4. 过载保护
    Test_Run("QT-007", "过载保护", Test_Exception_Overload);
    
    Test_PrintReport();
    
    // 生成测试结论
    printf("\n");
    if (g_test_suite.failed == 0 && g_test_suite.errors == 0) {
        printf("╔══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╗\n");
        printf("║                    ✅ 产品测试通过                           ║\n");
        printf("║                    可以出货                                  ║\n");
        printf("╚══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╝\n");
    } else {
        printf("╔══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╗\n");
        printf("║                    ❌ 产品测试失败                           ║\n");
        printf("║                    需要返修                                  ║\n");
        printf("╚══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╝\n");
    }
}

/*============================================================================
 * 运行所有测试
 *============================================================================*/
void Test_RunAll(void)
{
    printf("\n");
    printf("╔══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╗\n");
    printf("║                    运行所有测试                              ║\n");
    printf("║                    预计耗时: 30-60 分钟                      ║\n");
    printf("╚══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╝\n\n");
    
    Test_INA229_Driver_Suite();
    Test_Calibration_Suite();
    Test_RangeSwitching_Suite();
    Test_Accuracy_Suite();
    Test_Exception_Suite();
    Test_Boundary_Suite();
    // Test_Stability_Suite();  // 稳定性测试耗时很长，可选
    
    // 汇总报告
    printf("\n");
    printf("╔══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╗\n");
    printf("║                    所有测试完成                              ║\n");
    printf("╚══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╝\n");
}

/*============================================================================
 * 测试主函数
 *============================================================================*/
int Test_Main(void)
{
    // 初始化电流计
    CM_Init();
    
    while (1) {
        Test_ShowMenu();
        
        char input[16];
        if (fgets(input, sizeof(input), stdin) == NULL) {
            continue;
        }
        
        int choice = atoi(input);
        
        switch (choice) {
            case 1:
                Test_INA229_Driver_Suite();
                break;
            case 2:
                Test_Calibration_Suite();
                break;
            case 3:
                Test_RangeSwitching_Suite();
                break;
            case 4:
                Test_Accuracy_Suite();
                break;
            case 5:
                Test_Exception_Suite();
                break;
            case 6:
                Test_Boundary_Suite();
                break;
            case 7:
                Test_Stability_Suite();
                break;
            case 8:
                Test_RunAll();
                break;
            case 9:
                Test_QuickTest();
                break;
            case 0:
                printf("退出测试系统\n");
                return 0;
            default:
                printf("无效选择\n");
                break;
        }
    }
    
    return 0;
}
```

```text
│ TC-EXC-008│ 电源波动                      │ 异常     │ 测量稳定             │
│ TC-EXC-009│ 极端温度响应                  │ 异常     │ 误差<2%             │
│ TC-EXC-010│ 长时间过载恢复                │ 异常     │ 漂移<1%             │
├──────────┼────────────────────────────────┼──────────┼──────────────────────┤
│          │      边界测试                  │          │                      │
├──────────┼────────────────────────────────┼──────────┼──────────────────────┤
│ TC-BND-001│ 最小可测电流                  │ 边界     │ 0.1μA可测           │
│ TC-BND-002│ 最大可测电流                  │ 边界     │ 5A可测              │
│ TC-BND-003│ μA档上边界                    │ 边界     │ 1mA误差<2%          │
│ TC-BND-004│ μA档下边界                    │ 边界     │ 0.1μA可测           │
│ TC-BND-005│ mA档边界                      │ 边界     │ 边界正常             │
│ TC-BND-006│ A档边界                       │ 边界     │ 边界正常             │
│ TC-BND-007│ 切档阈值μA→mA                │ 边界     │ 0.8mA正确切换       │
│ TC-BND-008│ 切档阈值mA→μA                │ 边界     │ 0.4mA正确切换       │
│ TC-BND-009│ 切档阈值mA→A                 │ 边界     │ 80mA正确切换        │
│ TC-BND-010│ 切档阈值A→mA                 │ 边界     │ 40mA正确切换        │
│ TC-BND-011│ 电压测量边界                  │ 边界     │ 3~24V正常           │
│ TC-BND-012│ 分辨率验证                    │ 边界     │ 满足规格             │
├──────────┼────────────────────────────────┼──────────┼──────────────────────┤
│          │      稳定性测试                │          │                      │
├──────────┼────────────────────────────────┼──────────┼──────────────────────┤
│ TC-STB-001│ 24小时稳定性                  │ 稳定性   │ 漂移<0.1%           │
│ TC-STB-002│ 温度循环稳定性                │ 稳定性   │ 温度系数<50ppm/°C   │
│ TC-STB-003│ 连续切档稳定性                │ 稳定性   │ 错误率<0.1%         │
│ TC-STB-004│ 电源开关循环                  │ 稳定性   │ 100次无故障         │
│ TC-STB-005│ 数据记录完整性                │ 稳定性   │ 无无效数据           │
└──────────┴────────────────────────────────┴──────────┴──────────────────────┘
```

```c
/*============================================================================
 * test_fixture.h - 测试夹具接口
 *============================================================================*/
#ifndef __TEST_FIXTURE_H
#define __TEST_FIXTURE_H

#include <stdint.h>
#include <stdbool.h>

/*============================================================================
 * 测试夹具接口 (需要根据实际硬件实现)
 *============================================================================*/

// 电流源控制
void TestFixture_Init(void);
void TestFixture_SetCurrent(float current_a);
float TestFixture_GetActualCurrent(void);
void TestFixture_SetCurrentRange(int range);

// 电压源控制
void TestFixture_SetVoltage(float voltage_v);
float TestFixture_GetActualVoltage(void);

// 负载控制
void TestFixture_ConnectLoad(void);
void TestFixture_DisconnectLoad(void);

// 环境控制 (温箱)

float TestFixture_GetChamberTemp(void);

// 电源控制
void TestFixture_PowerOn(void);
void TestFixture_PowerOff(void);
void TestFixture_SetSupplyVoltage(float voltage_v);

// 故障注入

void TestFixture_InjectOverload(bool enable);

// 测量 (高精度参考)
```
